shared_focus = {
	id = HSM_CYA_hide_the_fugitive
	icon = GFX_goal_skull_treaty2
	x = 37
	y = 0

	cost = 1

	allow_branch = {
		has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_branch_unlocked
	}

	available = {
		has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_branch_unlocked
		NOT = { has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_path_chosen }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_dead }
	}

	bypass = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_path_chosen
			has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_dead
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 20
	}

	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HSM_CYA_hide_the_fugitive"
		add_political_power = 35
		set_country_flag = { flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_identity_secured days = 90 }
	}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
}

		clr_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_assassination_launched
		set_variable = { HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_exposure = 0 }
	}

shared_focus = {
	id = HSM_CYA_restore_imperial_provinces
	icon = GFX_goal_AF23_shared_unite_the_herzland
	prerequisite = { focus = HSM_CYA_regency_prepares_for_herzland }
	x = 0
	y = 2
	relative_position_id = HSM_CYA_regency_prepares_for_herzland

	cost = 5

	allow_branch = {
		has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_countess_dominant
		NOT = { has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_dead }
	}

	available = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = HSM_CYA_herzland_ready_tt
			OR = {
				date > 1013.1.1
				var:382.owner = { has_country_flag = holds_griffon_capital }
			}
		}
	}

	bypass = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_empire_descendant_declared
			controls_state = 382
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = { factor = 10 }

	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HSM_CYA_restore_imperial_provinces"
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_state = 382 } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = 382.owner } }
		if = { limit = { STW = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = STW } }
		if = { limit = { FEA = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = FEA } }
		if = { limit = { PYT = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = PYT } }
		if = { limit = { ROU = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = ROU } }
		if = { limit = { YAL = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = YAL } }
		if = { limit = { ANG = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = ANG } }
		if = { limit = { KAT = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = KAT } }
		if = { limit = { BRZ = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = BRZ } }
		set_country_flag = HSM_CYA_can_claim_empire_descendant
	}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY_BONUS }

		has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_dominant
		NOT = { has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_dawnclaw_dead }
	}

	available = {
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = HSM_CYA_herzland_ready_tt
			OR = {
				date > 1013.1.1
				var:382.owner = { has_country_flag = holds_griffon_capital }
			}
		}
	}

	bypass = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = HSM_CYA_empire_descendant_declared
			controls_state = 382
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = { factor = 10 }

	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HSM_CYA_march_on_imperial_provinces"
		if = { limit = { NOT = { controls_state = 382 } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = 382.owner } }
		if = { limit = { STW = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = STW } }
		if = { limit = { FEA = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = FEA } }
		if = { limit = { PYT = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = PYT } }
		if = { limit = { ROU = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = ROU } }
		if = { limit = { YAL = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = YAL } }
		if = { limit = { ANG = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = ANG } }
		if = { limit = { KAT = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = KAT } }
		if = { limit = { BRZ = { exists = yes NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } } create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = BRZ } }
		set_country_flag = HSM_CYA_can_claim_empire_descendant
	}
